1024: def _run_blender_cleanup(root: Path, asset_id: str, generated_model: Path) -> Path:
1025:     config_path = root / "asset_pipeline" / "image_to_blender" / asset_id / "blender" / "blender_job_config.json"
1026:     config = json.loads(config_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
1027:     config["input_model"] = generated_model.relative_to(root).as_posix()
1028:     config_path.write_text(json.dumps(config, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
1029:     output_dir = root / "asset_pipeline" / "unity_ready" / asset_id
1030:     output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
1031:     blender = shutil.which("blender") or "/opt/homebrew/bin/blender"
1032:     proc = subprocess.run(
1033:         [blender, "--background", "--python", str(root / "tools" / "asset_forge" / "blender_image_to_unity_cleanup.py"), "--", str(config_path)],
1034:         cwd=root,
1035:         text=True,
1036:         capture_output=True,
1037:         check=False,
1038:         timeout=300,
1039:     )
1040:     (output_dir / "blender_cleanup_stdout.log").write_text(proc.stdout, encoding="utf-8")
1041:     (output_dir / "blender_cleanup_stderr.log").write_text(proc.stderr, encoding="utf-8")
1042:     if proc.returncode != 0:
1043:         raise CompanyError(f"Blender cleanup failed: {(proc.stderr or proc.stdout).strip()[-800:]}")
1044:     receipt = output_dir / "blender_cleanup_receipt.json"
1045:     if not receipt.exists():
1046:         raise CompanyError("Blender cleanup receipt missing")
1047:     return receipt

1068: def _update_generated_asset_index(root: Path, asset_id: str, generated_model: Path, unity_ready: Path) -> None:
1069:     index = root / "asset_pipeline" / "index.json"
1070:     data = {"assets": []}
1071:     if index.exists():
1072:         data = json.loads(index.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
1073:     assets = data.setdefault("assets", [])
1074:     target = next((item for item in assets if item.get("id") == asset_id), None)
1075:     if target is None:
1076:         target = {"id": asset_id, "created_at": now_iso()}
1077:         assets.append(target)
1078:     target.update(
1079:         {
1080:             "status": "generated",
1081:             "image_to_blender_status": "model_generated",
1082:             "generated_model": generated_model.relative_to(root).as_posix(),
1083:             "unity_ready_model": unity_ready.relative_to(root).as_posix(),
1084:             "updated_at": now_iso(),
1085:         }
1086:     )
1087:     index.write_text(json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")

1315: def _receipt(
1316:     root: Path,
1317:     asset_id: str,
1318:     provider: str,
1319:     base: Path,
1320:     *,
1321:     gate_a_passed: bool,
1322:     gate_b_passed: bool,
1323: ) -> str:
1324:     rel_base = base.relative_to(root).as_posix()
1325:     status = (
1326:         "image_to_blender_ready"
1327:         if gate_b_passed

1360: def _update_asset_index(
1361:     root: Path,
1362:     asset_id: str,
1363:     provider: str,
1364:     prompt: str,
1365:     base: Path,
1366:     receipt: Path,
1367:     *,
1368:     gate_a_passed: bool,
1369:     gate_b_passed: bool,
1370: ) -> None:
1371:     index = root / "asset_pipeline" / "index.json"
1372:     data = {"assets": []}
1373:     if index.exists():
1374:         data = json.loads(index.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
1375:     assets = data.setdefault("assets", [])
1376:     target = next((item for item in assets if item.get("id") == asset_id), None)
1377:     if target is None:
1378:         target = {"id": asset_id, "created_at": now_iso()}
1379:         assets.append(target)
1380:     target.update(
1381:         {
1382:             "image_to_blender_status": (
1383:                 "image_to_blender_ready"
1384:                 if gate_b_passed
1385:                 else "waiting_for_gate_b"
1386:                 if gate_a_passed
1387:                 else "waiting_for_gate_a"
1388:             ),
1389:             "provider": provider,
1390:             "prompt": prompt,
1391:             "image_to_blender_job": (base / "image3d_job.json").relative_to(root).as_posix(),
1392:             "image_to_blender_receipt": receipt.relative_to(root).as_posix(),
1393:             "source_license": "pending_generated_or_project_owned",
1394:             "updated_at": now_iso(),
1395:         }
1396:     )
1397:     index.write_text(json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")

 320: def _clean_asset_id(asset_id: str) -> str:
 321:     clean = re.sub(r"[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]+", "_", asset_id.strip()).strip("_").lower()
 322:     if not clean:
 323:         raise CompanyError("asset id required")
 324:     return clean

 327: def _provider_order(requested: str, job_provider: str) -> list[str]:
 328:     if requested == "local":
 329:         return []
 330:     if requested == "auto":
 331:         provider = (job_provider or "trellis2").lower()
 332:         if provider == "both":
 333:             return ["rodin25", "pixal3d", "tripo", "trellis2"]
 334:         if provider in {"rodin25", "pixal3d", "tripo", "trellis2"}:
 335:             return [provider]
 336:         return ["rodin25", "pixal3d", "tripo", "trellis2"]
 337:     if requested == "both":
 338:         return ["rodin25", "pixal3d", "tripo", "trellis2"]
 339:     return [requested]

 342: def _resolve_source_image(root: Path, base: Path, job: dict) -> Path | None:
 343:     candidates: list[Path] = []
 344:     source_text = (job.get("source_image") or "").strip()
 345:     if source_text:
 346:         source_path = Path(source_text)
 347:         candidates.append(source_path if source_path.is_absolute() else root / source_path)
 348:     candidates.append(base / "source" / "concept.png")
 349:     candidates.append(base / "source" / "concept.jpg")
 350:     candidates.append(base / "source" / "concept.jpeg")
 351:     for candidate in candidates:
 352:         if candidate.exists():
 353:             return candidate
 354:     return None

 302:     def _complete_gate_a(
 303:         self,
 304:         asset_id: str,
 305:         source_path: str,
 306:         *,
 307:         approve: bool = True,
 308:     ) -> Path:
 309:         self._track(asset_id)
 310:         manifest = asset_gate_a_init(
 311:             self.root,
 312:             asset_id,
 313:             source_path=source_path,
 314:         )
 315:         evidence = self.root / f"docs/{asset_id}_source_rights.md"
 316:         provenance = self.root / f"docs/{asset_id}_provenance.md"
 317:         disclosure = self.root / f"docs/{asset_id}_disclosure.md"
 318:         snapshot = self.root / f"docs/{asset_id}_cc0_snapshot.md"
 319:         for path in (evidence, provenance, disclosure, snapshot):
 320:             path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 321:             path.write_text(f"Test evidence for {asset_id}.\n", encoding="utf-8")
 322:
 323:         relative_evidence = evidence.relative_to(self.root).as_posix()
 324:         data = self._read(manifest)
 325:         data.update(
 326:             {
 327:                 "task_id": "task-0011",
 328:                 "applicable_jurisdiction": "Republic of Korea",
 329:                 "provider_or_source": "Test Source",
 330:                 "creator_or_affirmer": "Test Creator",
 331:                 "rights_holder_or_legal_customer": "Channel Play Test Organization",
 332:                 "license_or_agreement": "Test commercial rights instrument",
 333:                 "source_reference": relative_evidence,
 334:                 "retrieval_date": date.today().isoformat(),
 335:                 "controlling_terms_reference": relative_evidence,
 336:                 "rights": {field: "PASS" for field in data["rights"]},
 337:                 "input_clearance": {
 338:                     "project_owned_or_cleared_inputs": True,
 339:                     "unverified_web_references": False,
 340:                     "watermarks_or_logos": False,
 341:                     "copied_franchise_style": False,
 342:                     "tracing_or_image_conditioning": False,
 343:                 },
 344:                 "evidence_paths": [relative_evidence],
 345:                 "critic_review": {
 346:                     "receipt": f"reviews/{asset_id}_gate_a.json",
 347:                 },
 348:             }
 349:         )
 350:         if source_path == "commissioned_human":
 351:             data["path_details"] = {
 352:                 "contracting_parties": "Test Organization and Test Artist",
 353:                 "signed_rights_instrument": relative_evidence,
 354:                 "downstream_asset_creation_grant": True,
 355:             }
 356:         elif source_path == "openai":
 357:             data["controlling_terms_reference"] = (
 358:                 "https://openai.com/policies/services-agreement/"
 359:             )
 360:             data["path_details"] = {
 361:                 "product_account_type": "api_business",
 362:                 "workspace_owner": "Channel Play Test Organization",
 363:                 "intended_model": "gpt-image-test",
 364:                 "beta_service": False,
 365:                 "beta_risk_acceptance": "",
 366:                 "producing_contributor": "Test Contributor",
 367:                 "contributor_transfer_evidence": relative_evidence,
 368:                 "input_rights_evidence": relative_evidence,
 369:                 "non_uniqueness_acknowledged": True,
 370:                 "human_review_required": True,
 371:                 "output_allocation_not_warranty": True,
 372:                 "indemnity_limits_reviewed": True,
 373:                 "ai_provenance_record": provenance.relative_to(self.root).as_posix(),
 374:                 "disclosure_decision_record": disclosure.relative_to(self.root).as_posix(),
 375:                 "public_service_sharing": False,
 376:                 "third_party_app_or_gpt": False,
 377:                 "likeness_or_real_person": False,
 378:             }
 379:         else:
 380:             data["source_reference"] = (
 381:                 "https://example.org/assets/truth-pen-source"
 382:             )
 383:             data["controlling_terms_reference"] = (
 384:                 "https://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/zero/1.0/legalcode.en"
 385:             )
 386:             data["path_details"] = {
 387:                 "exact_work_url": "https://example.org/assets/truth-pen-source",
 388:                 "original_host": "example.org",
 389:                 "cc0_marking_url": (
 390:                     "https://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/zero/1.0/"
 391:                 ),
 392:                 "retrieval_snapshot": snapshot.relative_to(self.root).as_posix(),
 393:                 "affirmer_authority_evidence": relative_evidence,
 394:             }
 395:         self._write(manifest, data)
 396:         if approve:
 397:             self._write_critic_approval(
 398:                 manifest,
 399:                 asset_id,
 400:                 task_id="task-0011",
 401:                 gate="A",
 402:                 receipt=data["critic_review"]["receipt"],
 403:             )
 404:         return manifest

 406:     def _complete_gate_b(self, asset_id: str, *, provider: str) -> Path:
 407:         self._complete_gate_a(asset_id, "openai")
 408:         asset_gate_a_check(self.root, asset_id)
 409:         manifest = asset_gate_b_init(self.root, asset_id)
 410:         source = self.root / f"asset_pipeline/incoming_2d/{asset_id}/concept.png"
 411:         source.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 412:         source.write_bytes(b"approved source")
 413:         records = {}
 414:         for name in ("prompt", "edits", "clearance", "disclosure"):
 415:             path = self.root / f"docs/{asset_id}_{name}.md"
 416:             path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 417:             path.write_text(f"{name} record\n", encoding="utf-8")
 418:             records[name] = path.relative_to(self.root).as_posix()
 419:         data = self._read(manifest)
 420:         data.update(
 421:             {
 422:                 "task_id": "task-0012",
 423:                 "source_asset_path": source.relative_to(self.root).as_posix(),
 424:                 "source_sha256": sha256_file(source),
 425:                 "source_created_or_acquired_at": "2025-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
 426:                 "source_provider_or_tool": "OpenAI API",
 427:                 "source_model_or_version": "gpt-image-test",
 428:                 "source_job_or_seed": "test-job-1",
 429:                 "prompt_record": records["prompt"],
 430:                 "edit_history_record": records["edits"],
 431:                 "clearance_record": records["clearance"],
 432:                 "attribution_disclosure_record": records["disclosure"],
 433:                 "rights": {field: "PASS" for field in data["rights"]},
 434:                 "production": {
 435:                     "approved_3d_provider": provider,
 436:                     "allow_unreviewed_fallback": False,
 437:                 },
 438:                 "critic_review": {
 439:                     "receipt": f"reviews/{asset_id}_gate_b.json",
 440:                 },
 441:             }
 442:         )
 443:         self._write(manifest, data)
 444:         self._write_critic_approval(
 445:             manifest,
 446:             asset_id,
 447:             task_id="task-0012",
 448:             gate="B",
 449:             receipt=data["critic_review"]["receipt"],
 450:         )
 451:         asset_gate_b_check(self.root, asset_id)
 452:         return source
 453:
 454:     def _write_critic_approval(
 455:         self,
 456:         manifest: Path,
 457:         asset_id: str,
 458:         *,
 459:         task_id: str,
 460:         gate: str,
 461:         receipt: str,
 462:     ) -> None:
 463:         path = self.root / receipt
 464:         self._write(
 465:             path,
 466:             {
 467:                 "schema": CRITIC_APPROVAL_SCHEMA,
 468:                 "asset_id": asset_id,
 469:                 "task_id": task_id,
 470:                 "gate": gate,
 471:                 "manifest_sha256": sha256_file(manifest),
 472:                 "reviewer_role": "critic_reviewer",
 473:                 "reviewed_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
 474:                 "verdict": "APPROVED",
 475:                 "source_creation_authorized": gate == "A",
 476:                 "production_authorized": gate == "B",
 477:             },
 478:         )
 479:
 480:     def _track(self, asset_id: str, *, status: str = "briefed") -> None:
 481:         index = self.root / "asset_pipeline/index.json"
 482:         data = self._read(index) if index.exists() else {"assets": []}
 483:         if not any(asset.get("id") == asset_id for asset in data["assets"]):
 484:             data["assets"].append({"id": asset_id, "status": status})
 485:         self._write(index, data)
 486:
 487:     def _asset(self, asset_id: str) -> dict:
 488:         data = self._read(self.root / "asset_pipeline/index.json")
 489:         return next(asset for asset in data["assets"] if asset["id"] == asset_id)
 490:
 491:     @staticmethod
 492:     def _read(path: Path) -> dict:
 493:         return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
 494:
 495:     @staticmethod
 496:     def _write(path: Path, data: dict) -> None:
 497:         path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 498:         path.write_text(
 499:             json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n",
 500:             encoding="utf-8",
 501:         )

  13: VALID_FORGE_KINDS = {"zone", "background", "prop", "character"}

  16: def asset_forge_new(root: Path, asset_id: str, *, kind: str = "prop", prompt: str = "") -> Path:
  17:     clean = _clean_asset_id(asset_id)
  18:     asset_kind = (kind or "prop").strip().lower()
  19:     if asset_kind not in VALID_FORGE_KINDS:
  20:         raise CompanyError(f"Invalid forge kind: {kind}")
  21:     asset_gate_a_init(root, clean)
  22:     gate_a_passed = evaluate_asset_gate_a(root, clean)["passed"]
  23:     gate_b_passed = evaluate_asset_gate_b(root, clean)["passed"]
  24:     production_status = (
  25:         "waiting_for_model_runtime"
  26:         if gate_b_passed
  27:         else "blocked_by_gate_b"
  28:         if gate_a_passed
  29:         else "blocked_by_gate_a"
  30:     )
  31:     description = prompt.strip() or _default_prompt(clean, asset_kind)
  32:
  33:     forge_root = root / "asset_pipeline" / "forge" / clean
  34:     concept_dir = forge_root / "concept"
  35:     schema_dir = forge_root / "schema"
  36:     cube_dir = forge_root / "cubepart"
  37:     blender_dir = forge_root / "blender"
  38:     unity_dir = forge_root / "unity"
  39:     for folder in (concept_dir, schema_dir, cube_dir, blender_dir, unity_dir):
  40:         folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  41:
  42:     schema = _part_schema(clean, asset_kind, description)
  43:     job = {
  44:         "asset_id": clean,
  45:         "kind": asset_kind,
  46:         "status": (
  47:             "forge_ready"
  48:             if gate_b_passed
  49:             else "waiting_for_source_creation"
  50:             if gate_a_passed
  51:             else "waiting_for_gate_a"
  52:         ),
  53:         "created_at": now_iso(),
  54:         "prompt": description,
  55:         "pipeline": [
  56:             {"stage": "concept", "owner": "image_prompt_engineer", "status": "ready"},
  57:             {
  58:                 "stage": "image_generation",
  59:                 "owner": "asset_factory",
  60:                 "status": "waiting_for_gpt_image" if gate_a_passed else "blocked_by_gate_a",
  61:             },
  62:             {"stage": "part_schema", "owner": "technical_artist_blender", "status": "ready"},
  63:             {"stage": "cubepart", "owner": "gdx_ops", "status": production_status},
  64:             {"stage": "blender_cleanup", "owner": "technical_artist_blender", "status": production_status},
  65:             {"stage": "unity_import", "owner": "unity_architect", "status": production_status},
  66:             {"stage": "gameplay_binding", "owner": "unity_gameplay", "status": production_status},
  67:             {"stage": "qa", "owner": "qa_playtest", "status": production_status},
  68:         ],
  69:         "outputs": {
  70:             "concept_prompt": f"asset_pipeline/forge/{clean}/concept/gpt_image_prompt.md",
  71:             "source_intake": f"asset_pipeline/forge/{clean}/concept/source_intake.md",
  72:             "part_schema": f"asset_pipeline/forge/{clean}/schema/part_schema.json",
  73:             "cubepart_job": f"asset_pipeline/forge/{clean}/cubepart/cubepart_job.md",
  74:             "blender_cleanup": f"asset_pipeline/forge/{clean}/blender/cleanup_plan.md",
  75:             "unity_import": f"asset_pipeline/forge/{clean}/unity/unity_import_plan.md",
  76:         },
  77:     }
  78:
  79:     (forge_root / "forge_job.json").write_text(json.dumps(job, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
  80:     (concept_dir / "gpt_image_prompt.md").write_text(
  81:         _concept_prompt(clean, asset_kind, description, gate_a_passed),
  82:         encoding="utf-8",
  83:     )
  84:     (concept_dir / "source_intake.md").write_text(
  85:         _source_intake(clean, gate_a_passed),
  86:         encoding="utf-8",
  87:     )
  88:     (schema_dir / "part_schema.json").write_text(json.dumps(schema, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
  89:     (cube_dir / "cubepart_job.md").write_text(
  90:         _cubepart_job(clean, asset_kind, description, schema, production_status),
  91:         encoding="utf-8",
  92:     )
  93:     (blender_dir / "cleanup_plan.md").write_text(
  94:         _blender_cleanup(clean, asset_kind, production_status),
  95:         encoding="utf-8",
  96:     )
  97:     (unity_dir / "unity_import_plan.md").write_text(
  98:         _unity_import(clean, asset_kind, production_status),
  99:         encoding="utf-8",

 103:     receipt_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 104:     receipt = receipt_dir / "asset_forge_receipt.md"
 105:     receipt.write_text(
 106:         _receipt(root, clean, asset_kind, forge_root, gate_a_passed, gate_b_passed),
 107:         encoding="utf-8",

 110:         root,
 111:         clean,
 112:         asset_kind,
 113:         description,
 114:         forge_root,

 121:
 122: def asset_forge_state(root: Path) -> dict:
 123:     forge_root = root / "asset_pipeline" / "forge"
 124:     jobs = []
 125:     if forge_root.exists():
 126:         for job_path in sorted(forge_root.glob("*/forge_job.json"), key=lambda item: item.stat().st_mtime, reverse=True):
 127:             try:
 128:                 job = json.loads(job_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
 129:             except json.JSONDecodeError:
 130:                 continue
 131:             job["path"] = job_path.relative_to(root).as_posix()
 132:             jobs.append(job)
 133:     ready = sum(1 for job in jobs if job.get("status") == "forge_ready")
 134:     return {
 135:         "status": "ready" if jobs else "empty",
 136:         "jobCount": len(jobs),
 137:         "readyCount": ready,
 138:         "jobs": jobs[:24],
 139:         "stages": ["concept", "image_generation", "part_schema", "cubepart", "blender_cleanup", "unity_import", "gameplay_binding", "qa"],
 140:     }

 142:
 143: def asset_semantic_pack(root: Path, asset_id: str) -> Path:
 144:     clean = _clean_asset_id(asset_id)
 145:     if clean != "pyramid_temple_full_environment":
 146:         raise CompanyError("Semantic pack v1 is implemented for pyramid_temple_full_environment only.")
 147:
 148:     forge_root = root / "asset_pipeline" / "forge" / clean
 149:     forge_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 150:
 151:     semantic_labels = _pyramid_semantic_labels()
 152:     nav_points = _pyramid_nav_points()
 153:     interactables = _pyramid_interactables()
 154:     scenarios = _pyramid_test_scenarios()
 155:     job = {
 156:         "asset_id": clean,
 157:         "kind": "full_environment_map",
 158:         "status": "semantic_pack_ready",
 159:         "updated_at": now_iso(),
 160:         "outputs": {
 161:             "simulation_contract": f"asset_pipeline/forge/{clean}/simulation_contract.md",
 162:             "semantic_labels": f"asset_pipeline/forge/{clean}/semantic_labels.json",
 163:             "nav_points": f"asset_pipeline/forge/{clean}/nav_points.json",
 164:             "interactables": f"asset_pipeline/forge/{clean}/interactables.json",
 165:             "test_scenarios": f"asset_pipeline/forge/{clean}/test_scenarios.json",
 166:             "agent_eval_plan": f"asset_pipeline/forge/{clean}/agent_eval_plan.md",
 167:         },
 168:     }
 169:
 170:     (forge_root / "forge_job.json").write_text(json.dumps(job, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
 171:     (forge_root / "simulation_contract.md").write_text(_pyramid_simulation_contract(clean), encoding="utf-8")
 172:     (forge_root / "semantic_labels.json").write_text(json.dumps({"labels": semantic_labels}, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
 173:     (forge_root / "nav_points.json").write_text(json.dumps({"route": nav_points}, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
 174:     (forge_root / "interactables.json").write_text(json.dumps({"interactables": interactables}, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
 175:     (forge_root / "test_scenarios.json").write_text(json.dumps({"scenarios": scenarios}, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
 176:     (forge_root / "agent_eval_plan.md").write_text(_pyramid_agent_eval_plan(clean), encoding="utf-8")
 177:
 178:     receipt_dir = root / "runs" / f"asset-semantic-pack-{clean}"
 179:     receipt_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 180:     receipt = receipt_dir / "receipt.md"
 181:     receipt.write_text(_semantic_pack_receipt(clean, forge_root, receipt), encoding="utf-8")
 182:     _update_semantic_index(root, clean, forge_root, receipt)
 183:     return receipt

 185:
 186: def _clean_asset_id(asset_id: str) -> str:
 187:     clean = re.sub(r"[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]+", "_", asset_id.strip()).strip("_").lower()
 188:     if not clean:
 189:         raise CompanyError("asset id required")
 190:     return clean
 191:
 192:
 193: def _default_prompt(asset_id: str, kind: str) -> str:
 194:     if kind in {"zone", "background"}:
 195:         return f"{asset_id} playable game environment with modular structures, paths, landmarks, and interaction-ready props"
 196:     if kind == "character":
 197:         return f"{asset_id} stylized humanoid game character with clear silhouette and rig-ready body parts"
 198:     return f"{asset_id} gameplay prop with readable silhouette, distinct interactive parts, and collider-friendly shape"
 199:
 200:
 201: def _part_schema(asset_id: str, kind: str, prompt: str) -> dict:
 202:     if kind in {"zone", "background"}:
 203:         parts = [
 204:             "terrain_base",
 205:             "main_structure",
 206:             "entrance_gate",
 207:             "corridor_modules",
 208:             "room_chambers",
 209:             "staircases",
 210:             "pillars",
 211:             "wall_panels",
 212:             "interactive_doors",
 213:             "trap_mechanisms",
 214:             "cover_blocks",
 215:             "landmark_props",
 216:             "spawn_markers",
 217:             "collider_proxies",
 218:         ]
 219:     elif kind == "character":
 220:         parts = [
 221:             "head",
 222:             "torso",
 223:             "left_arm",
 224:             "right_arm",
 225:             "left_leg",
 226:             "right_leg",
 227:             "hair_or_headwear",
 228:             "clothing",
 229:             "accessory",
 230:             "rig_markers",
 231:             "collider_proxy",
 232:         ]
 233:     else:
 234:         parts = [
 235:             "main_body",
 236:             "base",
 237:             "interactive_handle",
 238:             "moving_part",
 239:             "detail_panels",
 240:             "material_slots",
 241:             "collider_proxy",
 242:         ]
 243:     return {
 244:         "asset_id": asset_id,
 245:         "kind": kind,
 246:         "global_prompt": prompt,
 247:         "cube_part_prompt": f"{prompt}. This asset contains the following parts: {', '.join(parts)}.",
 248:         "parts": [{"name": part, "gameplay_role": _part_role(part)} for part in parts],
 249:     }
 250:
 251:
 252: def _part_role(part: str) -> str:
 253:     if "collider" in part:
 254:         return "Unity collision proxy"
 255:     if "marker" in part:
 256:         return "spawn or gameplay locator"
 257:     if any(term in part for term in ("door", "trap", "handle", "moving")):
 258:         return "scripted interaction"
 259:     if any(term in part for term in ("arm", "leg", "torso", "head", "rig")):
 260:         return "character rig or animation target"

 319: def _cubepart_job(
 320:     asset_id: str,
 321:     kind: str,
 322:     prompt: str,
 323:     schema: dict,
 324:     production_status: str,
 325: ) -> str:
 326:     return "\n".join(
 327:         [
 328:             f"# CubePart Job: {asset_id}",
 329:             "",
 330:             f"Kind: {kind}",
 331:             f"Status: {production_status}",
 332:             "Target runtime: gdx1 or cloud GPU if local runtime is unavailable.",
 333:             "",
 334:             "## CubePart Prompt",
 335:             "",
 336:             schema["cube_part_prompt"],
 337:             "",
 338:             "## Input",
 339:             "",
 340:             "- Preferred: cleaned source mesh from image-to-3D or Blender blockout.",
 341:             "- Alternative: text-to-3D seed output if CubePart supports it in the active runtime.",
 342:             "",
 343:             "## Part Names",
 344:             "",
 345:             *(f"- {item['name']}: {item['gameplay_role']}" for item in schema["parts"]),
 346:             "",
 347:             "## Required Output",
 348:             "",
 349:             "- One mesh per schema part.",
 350:             "- GLB or FBX export.",
 351:             "- Part labels preserved in object names.",
 352:             "- No final Unity import until Blender cleanup passes.",
 353:             "",
 354:         ]
 355:     )

 358: def _blender_cleanup(
 359:     asset_id: str,
 360:     kind: str,
 361:     production_status: str,
 362: ) -> str:
 363:     return "\n".join(
 364:         [
 365:             f"# Blender Cleanup Plan: {asset_id}",
 366:             "",
 367:             f"Kind: {kind}",
 368:             f"Status: {production_status}",
 369:             "",
 370:             "## Cleanup Rules",
 371:             "",
 372:             "- Normalize scale to Unity meters.",
 373:             "- Set origin to gameplay-useful pivot.",
 374:             "- Name all objects with CP_ prefix.",
 375:             "- Keep collider proxies as COL_ objects.",
 376:             "- Merge only visual parts that do not need scripting.",
 377:             "- Preserve interactive and physics parts as separate objects.",
 378:             "- Export clean FBX to Unity-ready path.",
 379:             "",
 380:         ]
 381:     )

 439:             f"- Source intake: {rel_root}/concept/source_intake.md",

 449: def _update_index(
 450:     root: Path,
 451:     asset_id: str,
 452:     kind: str,
 453:     prompt: str,
 454:     forge_root: Path,
 455:     receipt: Path,
 456:     gate_a_passed: bool,
 457:     gate_b_passed: bool,
 458: ) -> None:
 459:     index = root / "asset_pipeline" / "index.json"
 460:     data = {"assets": []}
 461:     if index.exists():
 462:         data = json.loads(index.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
 463:     assets = data.setdefault("assets", [])
 464:     target = next((item for item in assets if item.get("id") == asset_id), None)
 465:     if target is None:
 466:         target = {"id": asset_id, "created_at": now_iso()}
 467:         assets.append(target)
 468:     target.update(
 469:         {
 470:             "status": target.get("status", "briefed"),
 471:             "forge_status": (
 472:                 "forge_ready"
 473:                 if gate_b_passed
 474:                 else "waiting_for_source_creation"
 475:                 if gate_a_passed
 476:                 else "blocked_by_gate_a"
 477:             ),
 478:             "kind": kind,
 479:             "prompt": prompt,
 480:             "source_license": "pending_generated_or_project_owned",
 481:             "forge_job": (forge_root / "forge_job.json").relative_to(root).as_posix(),
 482:             "forge_receipt": receipt.relative_to(root).as_posix(),
 483:             "updated_at": now_iso(),
 484:         }
 485:     )
 486:     index.write_text(json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
 487:

| 4. 차단 스캐폴드가 준비 상태 표시 | **미해결** | 위 HIGH finding 참조. |
| 5. lifecycle와 gate 상태 혼합 | 해결 | gate index 갱신은 lifecycle `status`를 덮어쓰지 않으며 전용 테스트가 통과합니다. |
| 6. malformed/missing 입력 부작용 | 해결 | 누락 검사는 index/receipt를 만들지 않고, malformed object는 결정적 FAIL receipt를 생성하며 lifecycle을 보존합니다. |

추가로 확인된 생산 조건:

- Gate B가 source의 정확한 저장소 상대 경로와 SHA-256을 검사합니다.
- 승인된 단일 3D provider만 허용하며 `auto`/`both`는 승인 provider와 일치할 수 없습니다.
- 비로컬 provider 실패 시 로컬 fallback을 호출하지 않는 회귀 테스트가 통과했습니다.
- 현재 Truth Pen Gate A는 `FAIL`, Gate B manifest는 없음으로 평가됩니다.
- 현재 manifest SHA-256은 receipt의 `3f1648…b435f`와 일치합니다: [gate_a_check.md](/D:/Temp/User/User/channel-play-truth-pen-gate-a-1fadc5bf/runs/asset-gate-a-truth_pen/gate_a_check.md:6).
- Truth Pen PNG/GLB/FBX 또는 생성 모델은 발견되지 않았습니다.

- 집중 테스트: `15 passed, 3 subtests passed`
- Company 전체: `86 passed, 14 subtests passed`
- 변경 파일: 없음
